\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT},\\\\n        {\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": user_msg},\\\\n    ]\\\\n    if answer is not None:\\\\n
